## Classification rules

- Repeated `page.tsx`, `route.ts`, `layout.tsx`, and `loading.tsx` basenames are expected in the Next.js App Router and are not duplicates by basename.
- Native icon/splash variants, compatibility redirects, re-export shims, historical migration mirrors, and distinct worker/service responsibilities are retained until their own dependency and runtime contracts are reviewed.
- A Git blob SHA is inventory evidence, not proof of semantic correctness or use. `REVIEW_REQUIRED` remains until imports, dynamic references, consumers, tests, permissions, runtime behavior, and deployment dependencies are traced.
- This matrix covers tracked files in the recursive Git tree. Ignored and untracked local files are outside GitHub's tree API and are not represented.
